The rabbit symbolizes purity, longevity and peaceful elegance: a calm, gentle and graceful expression and pose that show this. ### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Rabbit, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Rabbit as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Rabbit's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Rabbit and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Rabbit? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Rabbit, people may describe you as gentle, thoughtful, and diplomatic. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា សុភាពរាបសា គិតដល់អ្នកដទៃ និងចេះសន្តិវិធី។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។

Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Rabbit? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Today, Sunday, 1 November 2026, is a Rabbit day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rabbit. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Beloved companion of the Moon Goddess, the Rabbit symbolizes purity, longevity, and peaceful elegance. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Rabbit are gentle, thoughtful, and diplomatic. Rabbits value harmony and are skilled at avoiding conflict.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Rabbit? Your luck is strong today. Stay calm, gentle and graceful. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is red. Wear something red, or carry a small red item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southwest. When you start something new, face the southwest, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 4, 30, 416!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Rabbit's lucky days of the week are Monday and Thursday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Rabbit can be overly cautious and avoid necessary confrontation. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: red. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ជាមនុស្សសុភាពរាបសា

13
00:00:32,857 --> 00:00:35,328
គិតដល់អ្នកដទៃ និងចេះសន្តិវិធី។

14
00:00:35,378 --> 00:00:37,513
ថោះឱ្យតម្លៃលើភាពសុខដុមរមនា

15
00:00:37,563 --> 00:00:39,950
និងជំនាញក្នុងការចៀសវាងទំនាស់។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,172
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

18
00:00:44,222 --> 00:00:46,839
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,069
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារ ថ្ងៃនេះ

21
00:00:52,119 --> 00:00:54,056
សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ និងសប្បុរស។

22
00:00:54,106 --> 00:00:56,440
ពាក្យសម្ដីល្អ ស្នាមញញឹម ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ

23
00:00:56,490 --> 00:00:58,361
អាចនាំឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ

24
00:00:58,411 --> 00:00:59,950
នឹងមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់។

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,905
សម្រាប់ការងារ និងការសិក្សា

26
00:01:01,955 --> 00:01:04,687
សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ឲ្យបានច្បាស់លាស់។

27
00:01:04,737 --> 00:01:06,642
សូមធ្វើរឿងសំខាន់បំផុតមុនគេ

28
00:01:06,692 --> 00:01:09,950
ហើយកុំខ្មាសក្នុងការសុំជំនួយ ពេលអ្នកត្រូវការ។

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:11,932
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

30
00:01:11,982 --> 00:01:15,175
សូមចាយវាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ និងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

31
00:01:15,225 --> 00:01:16,797
សូមគិតគូរមុននឹងចាយ

32
00:01:16,847 --> 00:01:19,950
ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,941
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ក្រហម។

34
00:01:22,991 --> 00:01:26,275
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌ក្រហម ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ក្រហមមួយ

35
00:01:26,325 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,380
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

37
00:01:32,430 --> 00:01:34,623
គឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

38
00:01:34,673 --> 00:01:37,333
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

39
00:01:37,383 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

40
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,867

41
00:01:42,917 --> 00:01:46,533

42
00:01:46,583 --> 00:01:49,950
ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

43
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677

44
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

45
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950
ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ 4 30 416!

46
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,020
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំថោះ

47
00:02:03,070 --> 00:02:05,652
គឺថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍។

48
00:02:05,702 --> 00:02:07,318
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

49
00:02:07,368 --> 00:02:09,950
សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

50
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,229
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ

51
00:02:12,279 --> 00:02:14,288
ជួនកាលប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេក

52
00:02:14,338 --> 00:02:16,568
និងចៀសវាងការប្រឈមមុខដែលចាំបាច់។

53
00:02:16,618 --> 00:02:18,332

54

និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

55
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

56
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

57
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

58
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

59
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

60
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

61

ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

62
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

63
00:02:41,707 --> 00:02:43,202
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

64
00:02:43,252 --> 00:02:45,641
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

65
00:02:45,691 --> 00:02:47,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

66
00:02:47,236 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 4 លេខ 30 និងលេខ 416។

67
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

68
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

69
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

70
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

71
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 character pictures as ingredients (each sign's clips use its own picture). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Ordinary Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Ordinary Day
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Ordinary Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Great Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Ordinary Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Ordinary Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Ordinary Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Good Day
3:20 Monkey (វក) – Ordinary Day
3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Take It Easy
4:00 Dog (ច) – Ordinary Day
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Good Day
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Curiosity
   English: Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានបីឆ្នាំដែលមានសំណាងល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំដែលគួរសម្រាក។ តើឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នក នៅត្រង់ណា?
2. Question
   English: Which of the twelve zodiac signs is the luckiest today?
   Khmer: តើរាសីណា ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ?
3. Practical
   English: Your lucky color, best time, and direction for today, for all twelve signs, in about five minutes.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden rabbit bring the golden kite home before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, a strong wind snapped its string, and the golden kite flew far away. Without it, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden rabbit. Today was a Rabbit day, and the rabbit felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down. "Little Rabbit," she said, "only you can bring the golden kite home. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Beloved companion of the Moon Goddess, the Rabbit symbolizes purity, longevity, and peaceful elegance. Pich smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring the golden kite home, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the rabbit, and it set off on its adventure, with Pich right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Rabbit are gentle, thoughtful, and diplomatic. Rabbits value harmony and are skilled at avoiding conflict.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was red, the rabbit's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The rabbit tied a red ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Pich, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the rabbit's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The rabbit stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Rabbit can be overly cautious and avoid necessary confrontation.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Pich came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the rabbit hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Pich. For the Rabbit today, that is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the rabbit rang the golden bells 4 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The rabbit climbed 30 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the golden kite, safe and shining, caught on a stone lotus. Hooray!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the kite, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Monday and Thursday shone brightest, the Rabbit's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the kite carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Pich right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The rabbit tied the kite to its pole and let it fly. For a moment, it wobbled. Then, whoosh! It soared up high, shining gold!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The golden kite shone in the sky again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The rabbit felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Rabbit, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: red. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Pich fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ជាមនុស្សសុភាពរាបសា គិតដល់អ្នកដទៃ និងចេះសន្តិវិធី។ ថោះឱ្យតម្លៃលើភាពសុខដុមរមនា និងជំនាញក្នុងការចៀសវាងទំនាស់។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌ក្រហម ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ទន្សាយថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ទន្សាយចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ក្រហម ជុំវិញក។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។"

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសនិរតី ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ទន្សាយ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែទន្លេធំមួយ បាំងផ្លូវ ហើយមានតែដុំថ្មតូចៗ សម្រាប់ឈានឆ្លង។ ទន្សាយឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ជួនកាលប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេក និងចៀសវាងការប្រឈមមុខដែលចាំបាច់។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ពេជ្រមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ទន្សាយលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។ ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ទន្សាយថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី រសៀល។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ទន្សាយវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនបួន ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ទន្សាយឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនសាមសិប កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញខ្លែងមាស នៅមានសុវត្ថិភាព និងភ្លឺចែងចាំង ជាប់នៅលើផ្កាឈូកថ្ម។ អស្ចារ្យណាស់!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញខ្លែង មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំថោះ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ខ្លែងយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ទន្សាយចងខ្លែងទៅនឹងបង្គោល ហើយបង្ហោះវា។ មួយសន្ទុះ វាយោលទៅមក។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! វាហោះឡើងខ្ពស់ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ខ្លែងមាស ភ្លឺលើមេឃម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ទន្សាយមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី រសៀល។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខបួន លេខសាមសិប និងលេខបួនរយដប់ប្រាំមួយ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

34
00:01:22,222 --> 00:01:24,077
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

35
00:01:24,127 --> 00:01:26,458
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

36
00:01:26,508 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌ក្រហម ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ទន្សាយថ្ងៃនេះ។

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,847
ទន្សាយចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ក្រហម ជុំវិញក។

38
00:01:32,897 --> 00:01:36,492
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

39
00:01:36,542 --> 00:01:38,829
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

40
00:01:38,879 --> 00:01:39,950
"

41
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,747
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

42
00:01:42,797 --> 00:01:44,357
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

43
00:01:44,407 --> 00:01:46,306
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

44
00:01:46,356 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសនិរតី ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ទន្សាយ។

45
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,774
ប៉ុន្តែទន្លេធំមួយ បាំងផ្លូវ

46
00:01:51,824 --> 00:01:54,072
ហើយមានតែដុំថ្មតូចៗ សម្រាប់ឈានឆ្លង។

47
00:01:54,122 --> 00:01:54,882
ទន្សាយឈប់។

48
00:01:54,932 --> 00:01:57,855
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ជួនកាលប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេក

49
00:01:57,905 --> 00:01:59,950
និងចៀសវាងការប្រឈមមុខដែលចាំបាច់។

50
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,777
ពេជ្រមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

51
00:02:01,827 --> 00:02:04,373
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

52
00:02:04,423 --> 00:02:06,104
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

53
00:02:06,154 --> 00:02:08,796
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

54
00:02:08,846 --> 00:02:09,950
"

55
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,081
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ទន្សាយលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។

56
00:02:13,131 --> 00:02:17,223
ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។

57
00:02:17,273 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

58
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

59
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

60
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

61
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

62
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

63
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

64
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

65
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,696
ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

66
00:02:41,746 --> 00:02:44,474
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

67
00:02:44,524 --> 00:02:46,220
" សម្រាប់ទន្សាយថ្ងៃនេះ

68
00:02:46,270 --> 00:02:48,125
គឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

69
00:02:48,175 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,971
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ទន្សាយវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:53,021 --> 00:02:56,721
ចំនួន 4 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,771 --> 00:02:57,971
ទីង!

73
00:02:58,021 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,196
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,246 --> 00:03:06,003
ទន្សាយឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 30 កាំ

76
00:03:06,053 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:13,750
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញខ្លែងមាស

78
00:03:13,800 --> 00:03:16,750
នៅមានសុវត្ថិភាព និងភ្លឺចែងចាំង

79
00:03:16,800 --> 00:03:18,650
ជាប់នៅលើផ្កាឈូកថ្ម។

80
00:03:18,700 --> 00:03:19,950
អស្ចារ្យណាស់!

81
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,279
នៅជុំវិញខ្លែង មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ

82
00:03:22,329 --> 00:03:23,306
ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

83
00:03:23,356 --> 00:03:25,566
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

84
00:03:25,616 --> 00:03:27,416
ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍

85
00:03:27,466 --> 00:03:29,950
ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំថោះ។

86
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,650
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

87
00:03:31,700 --> 00:03:35,350
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ខ្លែងយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

88
00:03:35,400 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

89
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

90
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

91
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

92
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,804
ទន្សាយចងខ្លែងទៅនឹងបង្គោល ហើយបង្ហោះវា។

93
00:03:53,854 --> 00:03:55,888
មួយសន្ទុះ វាយោលទៅមក។

94
00:03:55,938 --> 00:03:57,346
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

95
00:03:57,396 --> 00:03:59,950
វាហោះឡើងខ្ពស់ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,172
ខ្លែងមាស ភ្លឺលើមេឃម្ដងទៀត។

97
00:04:02,222 --> 00:04:03,369
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,419 --> 00:04:06,702
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,752 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,070
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,120 --> 00:04:14,910
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:14,960 --> 00:04:17,870
ទន្សាយមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,920 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,707 --> 00:04:33,202
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

109
00:04:33,252 --> 00:04:35,641
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

110
00:04:35,691 --> 00:04:37,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

111
00:04:37,236 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 4 លេខ 30 និងលេខ 416។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,061
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

113
00:04:44,111 --> 00:04:47,506
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់

114
00:04:47,556 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

115
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

116
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

117
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

118
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

119
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-01-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:01,780
Can one little golden rabbit

2
00:00:01,830 --> 00:00:03,479
bring the golden kite home

3
00:00:03,529 --> 00:00:05,244
before the full moon rises?

4
00:00:05,294 --> 00:00:07,989
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

5
00:00:08,039 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

6
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,251
But this morning

7
00:00:11,301 --> 00:00:13,852
a strong wind snapped its string

8
00:00:13,902 --> 00:00:16,617
and the golden kite flew far away.

9
00:00:16,667 --> 00:00:19,950
Without it, the festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,711
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,761 --> 00:00:23,189
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,239 --> 00:00:25,725
and out came a little golden rabbit.

13
00:00:25,775 --> 00:00:27,274
Today was a Rabbit day

14
00:00:27,324 --> 00:00:29,950
and the rabbit felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,896
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down.

16
00:00:32,946 --> 00:00:34,834
"Little Rabbit," she said

17
00:00:34,884 --> 00:00:38,012
"only you can bring the golden kite home.

18
00:00:38,062 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,368
Beloved companion of the Moon Goddess

20
00:00:42,418 --> 00:00:44,198
the Rabbit symbolizes purity

21
00:00:44,248 --> 00:00:46,355
longevity, and peaceful elegance.

22
00:00:46,405 --> 00:00:47,140
Pich smiled.

23
00:00:47,190 --> 00:00:48,447
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,497 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,233
To bring the golden kite home

26
00:00:52,283 --> 00:00:54,438
the little hero needed three

27
00:00:54,488 --> 00:00:56,958
lucky treasures: its lucky color

28
00:00:57,008 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,090
"Let's go!" said the rabbit

30
00:01:03,140 --> 00:01:06,694
and it set off on its adventure

31
00:01:06,744 --> 00:01:09,950
with Pich right by its side.

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,711
People born in the Year of the Rabbit

33
00:01:12,761 --> 00:01:15,622
are gentle, thoughtful, and diplomatic.

34
00:01:15,672 --> 00:01:18,383
Rabbits value harmony and are skilled

35
00:01:18,433 --> 00:01:19,950
at avoiding conflict.

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223
The first treasure was waiting

37
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

38
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768
Only one shone brightest.

39
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:28,586
It was red, the rabbit's

40
00:01:28,636 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

41
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,808
The rabbit tied a red

42
00:01:31,858 --> 00:01:33,844
ribbon around its neck.

43
00:01:33,894 --> 00:01:36,853
"Wear your lucky color," said Pich

44
00:01:36,903 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

45
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,410
Next, they came to a crossroads

46
00:01:42,460 --> 00:01:43,680
with many paths.

47
00:01:43,730 --> 00:01:45,506
One path began to glow.

48
00:01:45,556 --> 00:01:47,648
It pointed to the southwest

49
00:01:47,698 --> 00:01:49,950
the rabbit's lucky direction.

50
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,608
But a wide river blocked the path

51
00:01:51,658 --> 00:01:52,814
with only small stepping

52
00:01:52,864 --> 00:01:53,769
stones to cross it.

53
00:01:53,819 --> 00:01:54,724
The rabbit stopped.

54
00:01:54,774 --> 00:01:56,432
Sometimes, people born in the Year

55
00:01:56,482 --> 00:01:58,241
of the Rabbit can be overly cautious

56
00:01:58,291 --> 00:01:59,950
and avoid necessary confrontation.

57
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,521
Pich came close and smiled.

58
00:02:02,571 --> 00:02:05,093
"Breathe slowly," she said.

59
00:02:05,143 --> 00:02:06,807
"Trust your heart.

60
00:02:06,857 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

61
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,997
Stone by stone, the rabbit

62
00:02:12,047 --> 00:02:13,887
hopped across the river.

63
00:02:13,937 --> 00:02:16,879
Each stone glowed gold under its feet.

64
00:02:16,929 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

65
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

66
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

67
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

68
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

69
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

70
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

71
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

72
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,500
"The door opens only

73
00:02:41,550 --> 00:02:43,826
at your best time," said Pich.

74
00:02:43,876 --> 00:02:45,376
For the Rabbit today

75
00:02:45,426 --> 00:02:48,245
that is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon.

76
00:02:48,295 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

77
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,488
When the time came

78
00:02:51,538 --> 00:02:54,907
the rabbit rang the golden bells 4 times

79
00:02:54,957 --> 00:02:56,873
its first lucky number.

80
00:02:56,923 --> 00:02:57,899
Ding!

81
00:02:57,949 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

82
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,078
The door opened

83
00:03:01,128 --> 00:03:03,559
and a stairway of light appeared.

84
00:03:03,609 --> 00:03:06,191
The rabbit climbed 30 glowing steps

85
00:03:06,241 --> 00:03:07,920
its second lucky number

86
00:03:07,970 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

87
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:13,315
At the top of the tower, they found

88
00:03:13,365 --> 00:03:16,488
the golden kite, safe and shining

89
00:03:16,538 --> 00:03:18,796
caught on a stone lotus.

90
00:03:18,846 --> 00:03:19,950
Hooray!

91
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,637
Around the kite, seven little lights

92
00:03:22,687 --> 00:03:25,547
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

93
00:03:25,597 --> 00:03:28,159
Monday and Thursday shone brightest

94
00:03:28,209 --> 00:03:29,950
the Rabbit's lucky days.

95
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,029
Time was running out.

96
00:03:32,079 --> 00:03:34,603
Holding the kite carefully

97
00:03:34,653 --> 00:03:37,376
the little hero hurried home

98
00:03:37,426 --> 00:03:39,950
with Pich right beside it.

99
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

100
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

101
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

102
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

103
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,933
The rabbit tied the kite

104
00:03:51,983 --> 00:03:54,165
to its pole and let it fly.

105
00:03:54,215 --> 00:03:56,231
For a moment, it wobbled.

106
00:03:56,281 --> 00:03:57,305
Then, whoosh!

107
00:03:57,355 --> 00:03:59,950
It soared up high, shining gold!

108
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,716
The golden kite shone in the sky again.

109
00:04:02,766 --> 00:04:04,560
Lanterns rose into the sky

110
00:04:04,610 --> 00:04:06,900
and the full-moon festival began.

111
00:04:06,950 --> 00:04:08,035
Everyone cheered

112
00:04:08,085 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

113
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,646
The full moon shone down warmly

114
00:04:12,696 --> 00:04:14,646
as if to say thank you.

115
00:04:14,696 --> 00:04:17,689
The rabbit felt peaceful and proud.

116
00:04:17,739 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

117
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,208
If you were born in the Year

118
00:04:22,258 --> 00:04:25,192
of the Rabbit, today is your day too.

119
00:04:25,242 --> 00:04:27,773
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

120
00:04:27,823 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

121
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,922
Let's remember today's luck.

122
00:04:31,972 --> 00:04:33,119
Lucky color: red.

123
00:04:33,169 --> 00:04:35,936
Best time: from 1 to 3 in the afternoon.

124
00:04:35,986 --> 00:04:37,837
Lucky direction: southwest.

125
00:04:37,887 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416.

126
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

127
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Pich fell

128
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

129
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

130
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

131
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

132
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

133
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

134
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Rooster (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Rooster, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Rooster clashes with the Rabbit day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Rooster as ingredients (the Rooster is the only animal in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the rooster hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Rooster? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Rooster, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.

3. Question

   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា នេះជាមូលហេតុ។

   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Rooster? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Rooster.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Rooster? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 1 November, is a Rabbit day, opposite the Rooster in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be overly critical or blunt with others. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Rabbit day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Rabbit, Goat and Pig are lucky today, and the Rooster should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Sunday, 1 November: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rabbit!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Beloved companion of the Moon Goddess, the Rabbit symbolizes purity, longevity, and peaceful elegance.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Rabbit are often described as gentle, thoughtful, and diplomatic.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
For love today, be warm and kind. A kind word or a small gift brings you closer.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time, and finish what matters most first.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
For money, keep it simple. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is red. Wear it, or carry something red with you.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Your best time is from 1 to 3 in the afternoon, and your lucky direction is the southwest.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Rabbit's lucky days of the week are Monday and Thursday. Keep them for plans that matter.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Rabbit people can be overly cautious and avoid necessary confrontation. Stay balanced today.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Next, the Year of the Goat: a Good Day! The Goat shares a lucky triangle with the Rabbit.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
Team up with a friend today. Shared plans go well.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
Your lucky color is purple, and your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
Your lucky direction is the east. Lucky numbers: 7, 29, and 237.

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Goat friends, stay close to the Rabbit people today. Good luck grows when you share it.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Next, the Year of the Pig: a Good Day! The Pig shares a lucky triangle with the Rabbit.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
A good day to meet people and start something nice. Keep smiling.

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
Your lucky color is yellow, and your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
Your lucky direction is the east. Lucky numbers: 2, 88, and 595.

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
Pig friends, stay close to the Rabbit people today. Good luck grows when you share it.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
Today, 1 November, is a Rabbit day, opposite the Rooster in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
Be careful today: you can be overly critical or blunt with others. Pause before you react.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 33 (5:20–5:30)
Year of the Rat: a steady day. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning.

Scene 34 (5:30–5:40)

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-01-yt8.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,161
One sign has a Great Day today, two have

2
00:00:04,211 --> 00:00:08,055
a Good Day, and one should slow down.

3
00:00:08,105 --> 00:00:09,950
Which one are you?

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,025
Today is a Rabbit day.

5
00:00:12,075 --> 00:00:14,761
Coming up: one Great Day sign

6
00:00:14,811 --> 00:00:16,931
two Good Days, one sign

7
00:00:16,981 --> 00:00:19,950
to take it easy, and all twelve.

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,346
Rabbit, Goat and Pig are lucky today

9
00:00:23,396 --> 00:00:26,459
and the Rooster should slow down.

10
00:00:26,509 --> 00:00:29,950
Find your sign in the chapters below.

11
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,674

12
00:00:31,724 --> 00:00:36,042

13
00:00:36,092 --> 00:00:39,950

14
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,518
Sunday, 1 November:

15
00:00:42,568 --> 00:00:46,436
a Great Day for everyone born

16
00:00:46,486 --> 00:00:49,950
in the Year of the Rabbit!

17
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,726
Beloved companion of the Moon Goddess

18
00:00:53,776 --> 00:00:56,583

19
00:00:56,633 --> 00:00:59,950
longevity, and peaceful elegance.

20
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,397
People born in the Year

21
00:01:02,447 --> 00:01:05,907

22
00:01:05,957 --> 00:01:09,950

23
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,464
Your luck is strong today.

24
00:01:13,514 --> 00:01:16,572
Say yes to good chances

25
00:01:16,622 --> 00:01:19,950
and share your happiness.

26
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:24,181
For love today, be warm and kind.

27
00:01:24,231 --> 00:01:27,642
A kind word or a small gift

28
00:01:27,692 --> 00:01:29,950
brings you closer.

29
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,145
For work and study

30
00:01:32,195 --> 00:01:35,682
take one clear step at a time

31
00:01:35,732 --> 00:01:39,950
and finish what matters most first.

32
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,283
For money, keep it simple.

33
00:01:43,333 --> 00:01:45,976
Plan before you spend

34
00:01:46,026 --> 00:01:49,950
and save a little for tomorrow.

35
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:54,175
Your lucky color today is red.

36
00:01:54,225 --> 00:01:59,950
Wear it, or carry something red with you.

37
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,904
Your best time is

38
00:02:01,954 --> 00:02:05,122

39
00:02:05,172 --> 00:02:09,950
and your lucky direction is the southwest.

40
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,437
Your lucky numbers: 4, 30, and 416.

41
00:02:14,487 --> 00:02:17,386
Comment your sign below

42
00:02:17,436 --> 00:02:19,950

43

44
00:02:23,846 --> 00:02:26,434

45
00:02:26,484 --> 00:02:29,950

46

47
00:02:31,667 --> 00:02:34,950

48

49
00:02:38,148 --> 00:02:39,950

50

51

52
00:02:48,161 --> 00:02:49,950

53

Team up with a friend today.

54
00:02:55,714 --> 00:02:59,950
Shared plans go well.

55
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:05,345
Your lucky color is purple, and your best

56

58

67

68
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:55,345
Your lucky color is yellow, and your best

75
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,251

76
00:04:23,301 --> 00:04:27,329

77
00:04:27,379 --> 00:04:29,950
Some days don't go to plan.

78
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,617

79
00:04:31,667 --> 00:04:34,533

80
00:04:34,583 --> 00:04:37,554

81

Be careful today: you can be overly

83

84

85
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,046

86
00:04:54,096 --> 00:04:56,215

87
00:04:56,265 --> 00:04:59,950

88

89

90

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes a new voice for every clip, so even with the same words the voice can drift a little between scenes. Two ways to keep Chanda's voice the same:
1. Most reliable – one voice-over for the whole video: make every scene with "Copy, no voice" (music only; in Chanda's on-camera scenes her lips still move). Then record the whole "Voice-over" text of that video in one go, yourself or with one text-to-speech voice (for Khmer, for example Azure's km-KH-SreymomNeural; for English, choose one voice and always use it), and lay it over the clips in CapCut, using the subtitle file to line it up. One recording = one voice from start to end.
2. Quicker – Flow's own voice: always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice" (never retype or shorten the voice text: it describes the exact same voice every time), make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, and listen to each clip; if one sounds different, make only that clip again.
Do not mix the two ways in one video.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. Setting: a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a glowing full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, floating lotus flowers, ornate pink-and-gold lotus details, magical sparkles and warm golden reflections. Cinematic lighting, rich colors, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, gentle slow camera movement, professional animated-movie rendering. The zodiac animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos.

## Scene 1 – Rabbit day (Chanda on camera)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda matches her reference image. Then: magical and powerful. An adorable golden rabbit with long shining ears, big sparkling eyes and a tiny pearl necklace stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it springs from the lotus in a huge magical leap, flying in an arc across the full moon with a sparkling golden trail. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. An adorable golden rabbit with long shining ears, big sparkling eyes and a tiny pearl necklace, an adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell and a chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Rooster, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A proud, adorable golden rooster with big bright eyes, a ruby-red comb and shimmering tail feathers curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The rooster matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

## Voice in Google Flow (spoken inside each clip)
Flow (Veo) can make the voice and music together with the picture. Add ONE of these lines to the END of each scene prompt: the English one for the English video, the Khmer one for the Khmer video. Each line is short enough for an 8-second clip.
Put the clips in this order in your editor: 1, 2, 3, 5 (lucky numbers), 4 (ending).
Voice style (keep it the same every day so the channel has one recognisable voice): always the exact same voice in every clip of this series: a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a friendly smile in her voice, a calm, steady pace (about 150 words per minute), even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room (no echo, no reverb, no whispering, no singing, and no change of voice, accent, pitch, speed or energy from clip to clip)
Khmer note: if Flow does not pronounce Khmer well, use the English version in Flow and record the Khmer voice-over below yourself or with a Khmer text-to-speech app.
